The proposed framework may support feed allocation, grazing management, and livestock marketing decisions under heterogeneous sensing environments.

</details>


### [278] [HERALD: Counterfactual Audits and Minimal Repairs for Proof-of-Retrieval Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06012)
*Zhuowen Liu,Bohan Cui,YinShang Guo,Yuting Wang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HERALD，一个用于审计搜索智能体奖励设计的离线框架。该框架通过相同的同题干预、区分候选可见信息与预言机信息，并在策略优化前枚举检测器契约，揭示了一个关键问题：高奖励分数并不必然意味着引用的证据确实被检索到。作者在四个Qwen3-8B模型池上验证，发现无标签的引用洗白攻击可以成功，而通过精确定位强化L（引用语料库中但未出现在检索证据中的段落），可以实现包含最小化的修复，且经验攻击成功率降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体的奖励设计混合了答案质量、引用依据、工具成本和反黑客条款，导致高分数并不意味检索到了引用的证据，且额外的惩罚可能相互抵消。作者希望引入一个系统性的离线审计框架来分离这些问题，并确定最有效的修复方案。

Method: HERALD框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 应用完全相同的同题干预；(2) 分离候选可见信息与预言机信息；(3) 在策略优化前枚举检测器契约。作者在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上构建四个Qwen3-8B模型池，进行完整的2^3消融实验，测试搜索删除、伪造ID和引用洗白等攻击。

Result: R0方案能拒绝搜索删除和伪造ID攻击，但无标签引用洗白攻击仍然成功。通过2^3消融实验确定的包含最小化修复R[L]（强化引用语料库但未出现在检索证据中的段落），实现了零经验攻击成功率，单侧聚类上界为0.50%。该修复在HotpotQA和2Wiki上通过EM非劣效性门控（但MuSiQue未通过），引用精确度提升2.02点，支持召回提升1.46点，未支持引用下降1.69。

Conclusion: HERALD成功分离了鲁棒评分、稀疏学习信号和策略迁移三个维度。R[L]修复既保持了答案质量（EM非劣效）又大幅降低了引用洗白攻击的成功率。然而，在攻击移除预言机支持ID惩罚时，更广泛的加固仍然脆弱，且检测器在58,368条训练轨迹中仅出现18次，表明自然L未被降低——这揭示了奖励修复与策略学习之间的深层张力。

Abstract: Search-agent rewards mix answer quality, citation grounding, tool cost, and anti-hacking terms; a high score therefore need not imply that cited evidence was retrieved, and added penalties can cancel. We introduce HERALD, an offline audit that applies exact same-question interventions, separates candidate-visible from oracle information, and enumerates detector contracts before policy optimization. On four Qwen3-8B pools from HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue, $R_0$ rejects search deletion and fake IDs, but a label-free citation-laundering attack succeeds. A complete $2^3$ ablation identifies targeted strengthening of $L$---citing a corpus passage absent from the retrieved evidence---as the observed inclusion-minimal repair: $R[L]$ has zero empirical ASR with a 0.50% one-sided cluster upper bound. The gap persists across pool rules, a visible BM25 attacker, and four models; broader hardening remains vulnerable when the attack removes an oracle support-ID penalty. Under strict 5M-token matched training evaluated on 256 paired questions per benchmark, $R[L]$ meets the EM non-inferiority gate on HotpotQA and 2Wiki but not MuSiQue. Equal-suite citation precision and support recall improve by 2.02 and 1.46 points, unsupported citations fall by 1.69, and laundering attackability falls on 2Wiki and MuSiQue. Natural $L$ is not reduced, and the detector appears in only 18 of 58,368 training trajectories. HERALD thus separates robust scoring, sparse learning signal, and policy transfer.

</details>


### [279] [From Economic Agents to Agentic Economies: A Systems Blueprint for Economic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06020)
*Jiale Han,Xiang Li,Jing Qian,Wenyuan Gu,Pin Gao,Ye Luo,Hongyuan Zha,Dacheng Tao,Benyou Wang,Lin William Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了经济世界模型（EWMs）的实施路线图，将EWM系统分为六级能力阶梯，从基于规则和LLM的自适应代理世界，到自进化代理、进化制度世界以及与现实观测对齐的仿真到现实经济孪生，并通过系统文献综述指出现有工作集中于低层级，为下一代经济仿真环境建设提供蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济世界模型旨在从内部模拟经济演化过程，通过建模异质代理、信念、行为以及市场与制度机制来产生总体经济动态。然而，现有研究缺乏系统性的实施框架，迫切需要一份清晰的路线图来加速这一领域的发展。

Method: 作者构建了一个六级能力阶梯框架来组织EWM系统：从固定规则代理世界，到自适应和LLM代理世界，再到自进化代理、进化制度世界、仿真到现实经济孪生。同时进行系统文献综述，对各层级现有工作进行分类和评估。

Result: 文献综述发现现有研究集中在较低层级的代理和仿真环境，而具备自进化代理、内生制度、持续经验对齐以及经过验证的经济机制的EWM系统仍然稀缺。作者发布了精选论文列表和相关资源以支持后续研究。

Conclusion: 通过将EWM议程转化为可实施的蓝图，本文旨在加速下一代经济仿真环境的发展，使其能够作为人类决策者的高保真沙盒，并为AI代理提供训练、规划、评估和安全基座。

Abstract: Economic World Models (EWMs) are generative economic models that simulate how economies evolve from within by modeling heterogeneous agents, their beliefs and actions, and the market and institutional mechanisms through which their interactions produce aggregate outcomes. This paper develops an implementation roadmap for building economic world models as generative engines in which heterogeneous agents act, interact, adapt, and co-evolve with markets and institutions, thereby producing economic dynamics from the inside. We organize EWM systems into a six-level capability ladder, from fixed rule-based agent worlds to adaptive and LLM-based agent worlds, self-evolving agents, evolving institutional worlds, and sim-to-real economic twins aligned with real observations. A systematic literature survey across these levels reveals that existing work remains concentrated in lower-level agent and simulation environments, while systems with self-evolving agents, endogenous institutions, persistent empirical alignment, and validated economic mechanisms remain rare. By translating the EWM agenda into an implementation blueprint, this paper aims to accelerate the development of the next generation of economic simulation environments that can serve as high-fidelity sandboxes for human decision-makers and as training, planning, evaluation, and safety substrates for AI agents. We release a curated paper list and related resources to support future research.

</details>


### [280] [Integrating Implicit and Explicit Relational Biases through Graph-Based Multiple Instance Learning: A Case Study in Skin Lesion Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06037)
*Rafał Buler,Jakub Buler,Maciej Bobowicz,Michał Grochowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双层关系框架用于图像分类，将隐式关系表征学习（卷积掩码自编码器）与显式结构建模（图神经网络）相结合，在ISIC-2018和ISIC-2019皮肤病变诊断基准上均取得优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系归纳偏置对于捕捉数据间的结构依赖至关重要，但现有方法在隐式表征学习与显式结构建模之间存在鸿沟，本研究旨在弥合这一差距，探索双层关系框架在图像分类中的潜力。

Method: 首先以EfficientNetB3作为基线；其次采用基于补丁的策略，通过卷积掩码自编码器进行自监督重建，学习隐式的补丁间关系；随后将学习到的嵌入组织成多种图拓扑（网格、随机、k近邻）进行显式关系建模，最终将隐式补丁建模与显式图消息传递（图注意力网络）相结合。

Result: 在ISIC-2018测试集上，基线平衡准确率为76.17%，加入隐式补丁关系建模后提升至77.12%，完整集成网格结构的图注意力网络进一步达到79.27%。在ISIC-2019上，隐式方法达到59.84%，隐式与显式结合达到60.67%，均优于基线。

Conclusion: 结合隐式补丁间建模与显式图消息传递的框架能有效提升皮肤病变图像分类性能，其中网格结构的图注意力网络表现最优，验证了双层关系建模的互补性。

Abstract: Relational inductive biases are essential for capturing structural dependencies among data. This study investigates a dual-level relational framework for image classification, bridging the gap between implicit representation learning and explicit structural modelling. We begin by establishing a baseline using an EfficientNetB3 architecture. To move beyond standard convolutional biases, we adopt a patch-based strategy, employing a convolutional masked autoencoder to learn implicit inter-patch relationships through self-supervised reconstruction. We then extend this approach by incorporating explicit relational modelling, organizing the learned embeddings into various graph topologies, including grid-based, random, and k-nearest neighbour structures. Experimental results on the ISIC-2018 and ISIC-2019 skin lesion diagnosis benchmarks show that combining implicit inter-patch modelling with explicit graph-based message passing yields the best performance. On the ISIC-2018 test set, the baseline model achieves a balanced accuracy of 76.17%, which improves to 77.12% with implicit patch-based relational modelling. The fully integrated grid-structured Graph Attention Network further increases performance to 79.27%. Similarly, on ISIC-2019, the implicit approach reaches 59.84% balanced accuracy, while the combination of implicit and explicit modelling yields 60.67%.

</details>


### [281] [When History Lies: Evaluating and Improving Tool Use under Misleading Multi-Turn Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06057)
*Xiaoqing Wu,Xingyu Fan,Feifei Li,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了工具调用代理在持久交互中因历史轨迹污染导致策略被劫持的问题。作者提出了一个配对基准bench（包含Original、Polluted和Oracle State三种视图）和一种基于Oracle条件教师策略的知识蒸馏方法，通过软监督将可靠的决策策略迁移到仅观察污染历史的学生模型中。实验表明该方法在Qwen3-1.7B上达到87.0%的平衡工具使用准确率，优于多种基线，并展现出良好的可扩展性和跨场景泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持久交互中，历史工具调用轨迹可能在结构和语义上仍然看似合理，但实际上已不再对当前请求具有权威性。这种"历史污染"会劫持模型已具备的正确策略（在Qwen3-1.7B上导致32.1%原本正确的决策被翻转），迫使模型重复使用已过时的实体或接口约定。因此，历史可靠性被确定为一个独立的工具使用瓶颈，需要专门解决。

Method: 1) 构建配对基准bench，包含Original、Polluted和Oracle State三种同步视图，确保系统策略、当前工具、最新请求和黄金下一动作保持一致；2) 设计十一种保黄金干预措施，分别隔离决策状态、实体绑定和接口执行的失败；3) 提出ours方法，通过软监督将Oracle条件化的教师策略迁移到仅观察污染历史的学生模型，监督信号作用于学生自身生成的前缀上。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上，ours达到87.0%的平衡工具使用准确率，显著优于Gold-SFT（66.3%）、Oracle序列蒸馏（82.3%）和离策略token蒸馏（85.0%）。8B教师将1.7B学生提升至91.9%，8B学生达到93.0%。训练后的策略还能迁移到干净历史、未见函数、独立重生成的评估上下文、外部工具使用基准和嘈杂的多跳问答任务中。

Conclusion: 历史可靠性是工具调用中一个独特且重要的瓶颈，可靠状态策略迁移是一种有效且可扩展的解决方案。该方法不仅在受污染的基准上表现优异，还能泛化到多种下游场景，为解决工具调用代理中的历史污染问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: Tool-calling agents infer task state from accumulated dialogue and tool traces. In persistent interactions, however, historical traces may remain structurally valid and semantically plausible after they cease to be authoritative for the current request. We show that such history can hijack a policy the model already possesses: on Qwen3-1.7B, pollution flips 32.1% of decisions that are correct under the original trajectory and frequently induces reuse of corrupted entities or interface conventions. We introduce bench, a paired benchmark with synchronized Original, Polluted, and Oracle State views that preserve the system policy, current tools, latest request, and gold next action. Eleven gold-preserving interventions isolate failures in decision state, entity binding, and interface execution across complete calls and non-call decisions. We further propose ours, which transfers an Oracle-conditioned teacher policy to a student observing only polluted history through soft supervision on student-generated prefixes. On Qwen3-1.7B, ours achieves 87.0% Balanced Tool-Use Accuracy, outperforming Gold-SFT (66.3%), Oracle sequence distillation (82.3%), and off-policy token distillation (85.0%). The method scales consistently: an 8B teacher raises the same compact 1.7B student to 91.9%, while an 8B student reaches 93.0%. The resulting policies further transfer to clean histories, unseen functions, independently regenerated evaluation contexts, external tool-use benchmarks, and noisy multi-hop question answering. These results establish history reliability as a distinct tool-use bottleneck and demonstrate reliable-state policy transfer as an effective and scalable solution.

</details>


### [282] [Signal or Spurious Cue? A Randomized Audit of Survey-Country Metadata in LLM Social Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06085)
*Yifan Lyu,Xinran Li,Jiaqi Qiao,Xiujuan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过记录内审计检验了调查国元数据对LLM预测个体响应的影响，发现验证过的调查国信息能显著降低Brier损失，但披露随机标签的均匀来源并不能可靠地削弱模型对随机标签的方向性采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查国元数据可能改善LLM对个体响应的预测，但当元数据被随机分配时，可能误导预测。作者希望测试：如果告知模型随机标签来自均匀且与记录无关的分布，是否能减少模型对随机标签的采纳程度，以及验证过的真实调查国信息是否确实能降低预测损失。

Method: 采用记录内审计方法，对比不透明随机标签与披露随机标签（声明其均匀、记录无关的来源）对LLM预测方向的影响。使用五个固定API模型、六个国家和七个开发选择的预测目标，通过独立人口锚点和真实人类答案衡量方向与后果。主要面板包含72条记录（审后），另有混合覆盖一致性面板作为验证。

Result: 主要面板中，不透明和披露随机标签均产生0.214的国家方向偏移；配对衰减几乎为零（0.0003，95% CI [-0.0157, 0.0166]）。验证国家信息使Brier损失降低0.040（95% CI [0.024, 0.056]），而随机标签的后悔值包含零。一致性面板中，披露随机标签的正向移动和验证元数据效用得以保持，但衰减效应仍不确定。发布的PROV-FORECAST数据集包含14,400对项目级概率分布。

Conclusion: 验证过的调查国元数据在所选目标上具有实际预测效用，但向模型披露随机标签的均匀来源并不能可靠地削弱其对随机标签的方向性采纳，提示需要更有效的干预措施来防止无信息线索对LLM预测的误导。

Abstract: Survey-country metadata can improve an LLM's forecast of an individual response when informative, yet the same cue may redirect the forecast when assigned at random. A within-record audit tests whether disclosing a random label's uniform, record-independent origin reduces its country-directed uptake, and whether verified survey country lowers held-out Brier loss. Independent population anchors and recorded human answers measure direction and consequence across five fixed API models, six countries, and seven development-selected targets. In the primary post-review 72-record panel, opaque and disclosed-random labels each produced country-direction shifts of 0.214. Paired attenuation was 0.0003 (95% CI [-0.0157, 0.0166]). Verified country reduced Brier loss by 0.040 (95% CI [0.024, 0.056]), while random-label regret included zero. A non-overlapping mixed-coverage consistency panel retained positive disclosed-random movement and verified utility, while attenuation remained uncertain. On the selected targets, verified metadata was useful in both panels, but disclosure did not reliably attenuate random-label uptake. PROV-FORECAST contains 14,400 paired item-level probability distributions from the corrected panel.

</details>


### [283] [ECHO: A Locally-Deployable Agentic Health Assistant with Temporal Memory, Safety Guardrails, and Speech Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06110)
*Abdulkadir Külçe,Alihan Esen,Cağla Fikir,Berke Kurt,Kuzey Arar,Gökhan Ercan,Faik Boray Tek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ECHO，一个可本地部署的对话式健康助手，整合了基于LangGraph的智能体聊天机器人（94.9%工具执行通过率）、两阶段混合安全层（88.8%准确率、90.6%不安全召回率）以及多模态语音评估模块（平均宏F1为0.652），全程在消费级硬件上运行，数据不出本地，符合GDPR和KVKK合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期慢性病护理需要一个既能提供持续对话管理、又保障患者数据隐私和安全性的本地化健康助手，现有解决方案在安全防护、多模态评估和本地部署能力上存在不足。

Method: 系统整合三个模块：1）基于ReAct循环和LangGraph编排的智能体聊天机器人，配备17个临床工具和时序知识图谱实现跨会话记忆；2）两阶段混合安全层，包括规则层（处理危机信号和越狱尝试，<1ms）和基于APPNP传播的符号图神经网络（分类临床意图边界案例）；3）多模态语音评估模块，结合Whisper声学编码和BERT文本编码的交叉注意力融合，评估情绪、抑郁和疼痛。整体实现为网页应用，可在消费级硬件上运行。

Result: 工具执行通过率达到94.9%（59场景基准测试），安全层在2,537条标注土耳其健康数据集上达到88.8%准确率和90.6%不安全召回率，优于Llama 3.3 70B等零样本LLM基线；多模态语音评估平均宏F1达到0.652。

Conclusion: ECHO证明了在消费级硬件上实现安全、隐私合规且功能完整的慢性病对话健康助手的可行性，本地化部署和严格的安全机制为其临床实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents ECHO (Enhanced Care \& Health Observer), a locally-deployable conversational health assistant for long-term chronic care management. ECHO integrates three complementary software modules developed under shared supervision as a unified system. The core module is an agentic chatbot built on a ReAct loop orchestrated via LangGraph, equipped with 17 clinical tools and a temporal knowledge graph for persistent cross-session memory; it achieves a 94.9\% tool-execution pass rate across a 59-scenario benchmark with GPT-5 Mini. A two-stage hybrid safety layer intercepts all incoming queries: a rule-based layer handles explicit crisis signals and jailbreak attempts in under 1ms, while a signed graph neural network (GNN) with APPNP-style propagation classifies boundary cases by clinical intent, achieving 88.8\% accuracy and 90.6\% unsafe recall on a 2,537-query annotated Turkish health dataset while outperforming zero-shot LLM baselines including Llama 3.3 70B. A multimodal speech assessment module combining Whisper acoustic encoding and BERT text encoding with cross-attention fusion estimates emotion, depression, and pain, reaching a mean macro F1 of 0.652. The full system is implemented as a web application that can run entirely on consumer hardware, with no patient data transmitted to external services, supporting compliance with GDPR and KVKK.

</details>


### [284] [Evaluating Investment Logic in Large Language Models: A Real-World Benchmark Towards Personalzied Financial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06108)
*Yuanhong Jiang,Jingjie Zou,Zhenghong Lin,Xusheng Yu,Qiqi Huang,Shuai Jia,Shijie Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InvestLogicBench，一个包含201,247条真实投资者决策记录的过程原生基准测试，采用P→E→R→D→O（画像→事件→推理→决策→结果）追踪框架，揭示现有金融LLM评估方法（静态问答或终端盈亏）的缺陷，并主张该框架应成为个性化、后果性智能体的数据系统接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资能力本质上是高度个性化的，同样的市场信息对不同投资者可能产生不同合理行动。然而现有金融LLM评估要么用静态问答（忽略主体能动性），要么仅看终端盈亏（无法区分盈利是理性、符合画像还是运气）。作者质疑社区是否在用错误的尺子衡量具有后果性的智能体。

Method: 构建InvestLogicBench基准，收集151位真实投资者的201,247条有记录的决策，每条数据实例化为P→E→R→D→O追踪：投资者画像、可观察市场事件、投资推理、可执行决策、延迟结果。数据发布涵盖画像构建、时点事件绑定、结构化逻辑、时间跨度、结果和复盘，支持理解、画像条件生成和端到端重放三种评测模式。

Result: 对四个领先LLM的评测显示：逻辑合理性得分接近4/5，但事件接地分数仅为0.8-2.8/5；收益与过程质量得分也不一致。这暴露了模型表面流畅但缺乏实际依据的推理，而仅看结果指标的评估会掩盖这一问题。

Conclusion: P→E→R→D→O应作为数据系统接口，需包含版本化画像、时间溯源、可检查的检索、决策台账和可重放的结果。金融领域是更广泛的个性化、后果性智能体的压力测试场景。

Abstract: Investment competence is inherently personalized: the same market evidence can justify different actions for investors with different goals, horizons, portfolios, and risk boundaries. Yet financial LLMs are evaluated either by static question answering or by terminal profit and loss. The former omits agency; the latter cannot reveal whether a profitable action was grounded, profile-consistent, or merely lucky. We ask whether the community is using the wrong ruler for consequential agents.
  We introduce \textsc{InvestLogicBench}, a process-native benchmark containing 201,247 documented decisions from 151 real-world investors. Each episode instantiates a \textbf{P$\rightarrow$E$\rightarrow$R$\rightarrow$D$\rightarrow$O} trace: investor \textit{Profile}, observable market \textit{Events}, investment \textit{Reasoning}, executable \textit{Decision}, and delayed \textit{Outcome}. The release includes profile construction, point-in-time event binding, structured logic, horizons, outcomes, and post-mortems, and supports comprehension, profile-conditioned generation, and end-to-end replay. Across four leading LLMs, logical plausibility remains near 4/5 while event grounding is only 0.8--2.8/5; return and process quality also disagree. These results expose polished but weakly grounded reasoning that outcome-only evaluation hides. We further argue that P$\rightarrow$E$\rightarrow$R$\rightarrow$D$\rightarrow$O should be a data-system interface, requiring versioned profiles, temporal provenance, inspectable retrieval, decision ledgers, and replayable outcomes. Finance is our stress test for a broader class of personalized, consequential agents.

</details>


### [285] [Poli-Bias: Understanding and Measuring Large Language Model Biases in International Political Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06123)
*Massi-Nissa Abboud,Aladin Djuhera,Elena Cabrio,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Poli-Bias框架，通过反事实对比实验系统地测量LLM在不同国家情境下的政治偏见，将响应差异分解为五个可解释维度，并在13个LLM上验证了国家身份和用户归属会系统性影响模型对国际法下等价行为的描述与评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以用单一指标捕捉LLM中微妙的政治偏见，这些偏见体现在措辞、论证方式和法律推理等细微差异中，需要更细粒度的测量框架。

Method: 采用反事实框架，通过系统性地交换配对提示中的国家身份，构建跨地缘政治关系、法律违规和推理任务的对比实验；将响应差异分解为五个可解释维度以揭示不平等待遇的具体表现形式。

Result: 在13个涵盖不同模型家族和规模的当代LLM中，发现国家身份和用户归属会系统性影响模型对国际法下等价行为的描述、评价和辩护方式。

Conclusion: Poli-Bias为审计LLM的政治公正性和谄媚行为（sycophancy）提供了一个细粒度的框架。

Abstract: Measuring political bias in large language models (LLMs) remains challenging as it can manifest through subtle differences in framing, argumentation, and legal reasoning that are difficult to capture with a single metric. In this work, we introduce Poli-Bias, a counterfactual framework for measuring whether LLMs treat legally equivalent conflict scenarios differently depending on the countries involved. Poli-Bias compares responses to paired prompts in which country identities are systematically swapped across diverse geopolitical relationships, legal violations, and reasoning tasks. Rather than reducing bias to a single judgment, our framework decomposes response disparities into five interpretable dimensions, revealing how and where unequal treatment manifests. Across 13 contemporary LLMs spanning diverse model families and sizes, we find that country identities and user affiliations can systematically affect how equivalent actions are described, evaluated, and defended under international law. Our results thus establish Poli-Bias as a fine-grained framework for auditing political even-handedness and sycophancy in LLMs.

</details>


### [286] [Schema-Guided Hierarchical Information Extraction and Semantic Evaluation Using Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06167)
*Modhurita Mitra,Jan-Willem Versteeg,Maarten D. Schermer,Shiva Nadi Najafabadi,Marie L. De Bruin,Lourens T. Bloem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于schema的框架，利用生成式AI从非结构化文本中提取复杂结构化信息，并通过路径匹配算法和生成式AI实现自动化语义评估。在NICE健康技术评估文档上，14个属性中有12个F1>90%，提取速度比人类专家快约30倍，且展示了跨模型、跨组织和跨语言的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非结构化文档中提取复杂、嵌套的结构化信息（如健康技术评估报告）是一项具有挑战性的任务，需要统一的框架来兼顾信息提取和结果评估的一致性。

Method: 1) 设计基于schema的信息模型，编码领域知识，支持层级嵌套和可变基数的属性提取；2) 单次零样本调用生成式AI完成文档提取；3) 提出路径匹配算法对齐嵌套的可变基数属性；4) 利用生成式AI进行语义比较，并引入精确/语义/有用/不匹配的四级分类rubric。

Result: 在NICE文档上14个属性中12个F1>90%（使用Claude Opus 3）；提取时间比人类专家快约30倍；框架在不同生成式AI模型、不同HTA组织和不同语言上均表现出泛化性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该schema驱动框架为复杂结构化信息提取及其自动语义评估提供了统一、系统且高效的解决方案，在健康技术评估领域具有实际应用价值和跨场景通用性。

Abstract: We present a schema-based framework for extracting complex, structured information from unstructured text documents using generative AI, followed by automated semantic evaluation of the extracted information against a gold standard. The schema, serving as an information model encoding domain knowledge, provides a unified, systematic, and consistent framework for extraction of hierarchical, nested information, with attributes of variable cardinality, and subsequent evaluation of the results. Information extraction from a document is performed in a single call to the model, in zero-shot mode.
  In the evaluation step, we introduce a path-based semantic matching algorithm to align the nested, variable-cardinality attributes in the extracted results with those in the gold standard. We use generative AI for semantic comparison of the extracted and gold standard values of an attribute, and introduce a rubric to classify the result of the comparison, according to domain-specific considerations, as an exact, semantic, useful, or non-match.
  We were able to extract 12 out of 14 attributes with an F1 score of $>$90\% from documents published by the health technology assessment organisation NICE, using the generative AI model Claude Opus 3. The time needed to extract the attributes from a document was $\sim$30 times lower than the time taken by a human domain expert. We further demonstrate generalisability of this framework across different generative AI models and transferability across different HTA organisations and languages.

</details>


### [287] [Mind the Gaps: Mixture-of-Minds for Human Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06115)
*Pranav Dahiya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Anacreon，一个面向个体层面的受众模拟模型。它通过学习作者嵌入将人群分离，在真实质性语料上围绕种子人物聚类，并为每个聚类训练专用适配器（混合思维模型），基于Gemma 4 12B构建。该模型从公开文本中提取人口统计、心理特质和调查响应，并加入情绪链增强；通过打乱选项顺序降低提示脆弱性，通过平衡训练分布减少正向偏差。在外部大规模调查上达到0.775的序数对齐SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计方法和LLM模拟器在群体层面表现良好，但在个体层面失效：它们能恢复群体的中心趋势，却抹平了个体异质性，且带有社会偏见和提示脆弱性，导致个体预测失真。作者旨在弥合群体统计预测与个体忠实模拟之间的鸿沟。

Method: Anacreon采用三层架构：(1)学习作者嵌入以分离个体；(2)在真实质性语料上围绕种子人物进行聚类；(3)为每个聚类训练专用适配器（混合思维模型），基于Gemma 4 12B基础模型。从公开文本中提取人口统计、心理特征和调查响应数据，为每条记录添加情绪链（chain-of-emotion）；通过打乱响应选项降低提示脆弱性，通过平衡训练分布减少正向偏差。

Result: 在外部来源的大规模调查上，Anacreon达到0.775的序数对齐分数，这是领域公认的个体级准确度指标，达到当前最优水平，且残差偏差较小。

Conclusion: 该工作是向从忠实模拟的个体中提取群体洞见迈出的一步，为缩小LLM模拟器在个体层面的失真问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Predicting how a population will answer a new question is a long-standing goal. Statistical methods succeed at the level of the mass but falter at the level of the individual. Large language model simulators inherit this gap. They recover a population's central tendencies while flattening its heterogeneity, and they carry social biases and prompt brittleness that distort individual predictions. This paper introduces Anacreon, an audience simulation model that targets the individual level within a narrow, well-specified domain. Anacreon learns an authorship embedding that separates individuals, clusters a real qualitative corpus around seed people, and trains a dedicated adapter for each cluster, a mixture of minds, on a Gemma~4 12B base. It harvests demographics, psychological traits, and survey responses from public text, and augments each record with a chain-of-emotion. It reduces prompt brittleness by shuffling response options and reduces positive bias by balancing the training distribution. On a large, externally sourced survey, Anacreon reaches a state-of-the-art ordinal alignment of 0.775, the individual-level accuracy measure on which the field has converged, with a small residual bias. The work is a step toward drawing aggregate insight from faithfully simulated individuals.

</details>


### [288] [HarnessOpt-Bench: Evaluating LLMs at Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06301)
*Varun Ursekar,Apaar Shanker,Yash Maurya,Shehab Yasser,Vijay S. Kalmath,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessOpt-Bench，一个用于端到端"编排系统（harness）优化"的基准测试框架。在昂贵的随机评估条件下，评估LLM优化器能否通过迭代改进目标智能体的harness（包括提示、工具、控制流、内存和编排代码）来提升系统性能。实验涵盖5个前沿LLM在4个下游任务上的111次评分运行，结果显示优化器模型之间的差异大于其所使用的编码harness，且该能力仍有巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在智能体系统中的部署日益增多，系统性能不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于围绕模型的harness（提示、工具、控制流、内存和编排代码）。自动harness优化已成为提升AI系统的重要途径，但社区缺乏统一的协议来衡量前沿LLM在此任务上的表现，因此需要一个标准化的基准。

Method: 提出HarnessOpt-Bench框架：优化器（LLM+编码harness的配对）接收目标智能体的种子harness、分级评估反馈和固定评估预算；优化器编辑harness并提交最终候选；通过归一化收益（相对种子）在保留测试分区上评分。可信执行环境强制评估边界、监控目标智能体的资源使用并保留候选版本用于审计。实验在5个前沿LLM、4个下游任务上进行，共111次评分运行。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 优化器模型之间的差异大于它们所使用的编码harness之间的差异；(2) 原生harness并非一贯优于共享编码harness；(3) 不同任务和种子设置下的收益差异显著。这些结果证明harness优化是可测量且有区分度的能力。

Conclusion: Harness优化是一种可测量的、具有区分度的模型能力，但目前仍有巨大的提升空间。HarnessOpt-Bench为社区提供了标准化协议，可用于衡量和推动LLM在自动化harness优化方面的进展。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed within agentic systems, their capabilities depend not only on the model weights but also on the harness: the prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code surrounding them. This makes automated harness optimization -- the iterative and evaluation-guided improvement of a harness by an AI system -- both an important route to improving AI systems and a demanding capability for AI systems themselves. Yet the community lacks a common protocol for measuring how well frontier LLMs perform at this task. We introduce HarnessOpt-Bench, a benchmark for end-to-end harness optimization under expensive and stochastic evaluation. An optimizer, an LLM paired with a coding harness, receives a target agent's seed harness, graded evaluation feedback, and a fixed target-evaluation budget. It edits the harness and nominates a final candidate, which is scored by its normalized gain over the seed on a held-out test partition that remains inaccessible throughout search. A trusted execution environment enforces the evaluation boundary, meters target-agent resource use, and preserves candidate versions for audit. We evaluate 5 frontier LLMs as optimizers both under a shared coding harness and under their native harnesses across 4 downstream tasks, over 111 scored runs. Experiment results show that optimizer models separate more than the coding harnesses they act through, native harnesses are not consistently superior, and gains vary substantially across tasks and seed regimes. These results establish harness optimization as a measurable and discriminative capability with large space for improvement.

</details>


### [289] [Beyond Top-K: Replacing Black-Box Retrieval with Interpretable Agentic Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06305)
*Sagar Tamang,Ayush Vyas,Tabarakul Hazarika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对长文档（尤其是财务报表、审计报告等表格密集型文档）提出了一种名为READ（Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search）的检索增强生成方法，通过三个确定性操作（归一化词法搜索、结构导航、有界跨度读取）取代传统的chunk-embedding-top-k检索设计，在51个验证问题上以58.8%的准确率大幅超越稠密检索的15.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG对长文档采用"分块-嵌入-取top-k近邻"的设计，但对财务报表等结构化文档而言该设计存在结构性缺陷：86.8%的内容行是表格行，大量近似的数字在同一个嵌入空间中竞争，且数字的单位（如lakh/crore）可能来源于13行以上的表头，分块边界会割裂数字与其单位，导致两个数量级的错误。

Method: 提出READ方法：一个代理通过三个确定性操作读取原始文档——归一化词法搜索、结构导航、有界跨度读取，并通过Model Context Protocol暴露，使轨迹成为可重放的审计痕迹而非不透明的相似度分数。该方法不依赖嵌入，结合了词法检索的可靠性与代理的迭代推理能力。

Result: 在51个验证问题上，READ达到58.8%准确率，远超稠密检索的15.7%（p_Holm=2×10^-5）；即使稠密检索调优至35.3%，READ仍领先23.5个百分点（p_Holm=0.017）。配备top-k工具但同样迭代的代理仅达27.5%，表明增益来自接口设计而非迭代本身。BM25与READ在统计上无显著差异，证明结果区分的是基于嵌入与无嵌入的检索，而非代理与词法搜索。

Conclusion: 对于表格密集型长文档，基于嵌入的RAG设计存在根本性缺陷，而READ这种无嵌入的确定性代理检索方案能有效解决单位继承、表头分离等问题。研究还表明，性能提升的关键在于检索接口的设计（确定性操作vs top-k近邻），而非简单的代理迭代，这为长文档RAG提供了新的设计方向。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over long documents is dominated by one design: chunk the text, embed the chunks, and surface the top-k nearest neighbours of the query. We argue that for an important class of documents -- financial statements, audit reports, regulatory returns -- this design is structurally unsound, and we make the argument measurable. On a 780-page government financial report, 86.8% of content lines are table rows, thousands of near-identical figures compete in one embedding space, and a figure inherits its unit from a header a median of 13 lines above it -- so a chunk boundary routinely separates a number from whether it is in lakh or crore, an error of two orders of magnitude. A table-aware chunker built as a steelman fixes the unit problem but leaves 27-30% of numeric chunks with no fiscal-year header at every chunk size we tried. We propose READ (Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search), in which an agent reads the raw document through three deterministic operations -- normalized lexical search, structural navigation, and bounded span reads -- exposed over the Model Context Protocol, so a trajectory is a replayable audit trail, not an opaque similarity score. On 51 verified questions READ answers 58.8% against dense retrieval's 15.7% (p_Holm = 2 x 10^-5) -- or 35.3% tuned, which READ still leads by 23.5 points (p_Holm = 0.017). An agent given the same loop but a top-k tool reaches only 27.5%, locating the gain in the interface rather than in iteration. We also report what the evidence does not support: BM25 is statistically indistinguishable from READ, so our result separates embedding-based from embedding-free retrieval, not agentic from lexical search.

</details>


### [290] [Contextual Information Policy Optimization for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06128)
*Xingyu Guo,Wei Chen,Linlin Yang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文信息策略优化（CIPO）框架，一种面向证据导向的强化学习方法，通过为受检索信息影响的推理动作分配密集的回合级信用，同时结合全局结果奖励，以对齐策略优化与外部证据使用，减少先验驱动的推理和确认偏差。CIPO无需人工过程标注或额外奖励模型，在七个领域内外的基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体方法主要奖励最终答案正确性或中间进展，未直接评估检索后的动作是否基于检索证据，导致先验驱动推理：智能体基于内部知识预先形成结论，检索仅用于确认，产生确认偏差和低效的证据使用。

Method: 提出CIPO证据导向的强化学习框架：为受检索信息影响的推理动作分配密集的回合级信用（evidence-use信号），并将其与全局结果奖励结合以保持答案正确性。该方法不需要人工过程标注，也不需要额外的奖励模型。

Result: 在七个领域内和领域外基准上的大量实验表明，CIPO减少了先验驱动推理的发生，并在大多数任务上取得了优异的性能。

Conclusion: CIPO通过显式对齐强化学习与外部证据使用，有效抑制了证据脱离的猜测，促进了检索事实指导或修正后续推理的轨迹，在保证答案正确性的同时提升了证据利用效率。

Abstract: Search agents extend large language models beyond static parametric memory by enabling them to acquire and use ex ternal evidence during multi-step reasoning. For knowledge intensive tasks involving complex or evolving information, their reliability depends not only on retrieving relevant ev idence but also on using it to guide subsequent reasoning. However, existing methods primarily reward final-answer cor rectness or intermediate progress, without directly assessing whether post-retrieval actions are grounded in the retrieved evidence. This misalignment encourages prior-driven reason ing: agents form conclusions based on internal knowledge and use retrieval mainly to confirm them, resulting in confirma tion bias and inefficient evidenceuse.Toaddressthisissue, we propose Contextual Information Policy Optimization (CIPO), an evidence-oriented reinforcement learning framework that explicitly aligns policy optimization with external evidence use. CIPO assigns dense, turn-level credit to reasoning ac tions influenced by retrieved information, while combining this evidence-use signal with a global outcome reward to pre serveanswercorrectness.Withthismanner,CIPOdiscourages evidence-detached guesses and promotes reasoning trajecto ries in which retrieved facts can guide or revise subsequent reasoning. Importantly, CIPO requires neither human process annotations nor an additional reward model. Extensive exper iments on seven in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks show that CIPO reduces the prevalence of prior-driven rea soning and achieves excellent performance on most tasks.

</details>


### [291] [FinEvo-Bench: A Longitudinal Benchmark for Self-Evolving Agents in Professional Financial Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06144)
*Bo Deng,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Chongyang Tao,Xuanren Chen,Chenggang Xie,Renzhao Liang,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinEvo-Bench，一个包含120个真实案例、覆盖六个金融领域20个业务场景的纵向基准测试，用于评估智能体在专业工作流中的自我进化能力。实验对比四种智能体框架，发现进化条件平均提升9.33-19.37分，且技能进化优于记忆进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试独立评估任务，无法衡量经验积累对后续任务的影响；已有自我进化基准也未能联合覆盖专业工作流、开放性交付物和多维度评估，因此缺乏一个能同时测度专业表现与自我进化能力的基准。

Method: 构建FinEvo-Bench基准：120个基于真实案例的任务、20个业务场景覆盖六个金融领域；机构提供的专业流程定义操作和约束；每个场景含六个案例共享专业流程和人工审查的评分标准。使用相同的Qwen3.7-Max骨干对比四种自我进化智能体框架，三组独立打乱的任务流，配对非进化对照组估计进化增益，由独立的Claude Code评分代理评估输出。

Result: Letta取得最高进化分数（91.65）和最少的合规问题（每任务0.09）；Codex取得最大自我进化增益（+19.37）。进化条件整体提升9.33-19.37分，合规问题每任务减少0.12-0.44。场景内排名4-6的配对得分增益比排名1-3高6.10-8.70分。技能进化优于记忆进化和两者组合；rubric反馈优于参考答案反馈。

Conclusion: FinEvo-Bench能够有效度量智能体的专业表现和自我进化能力，即智能体将先前经验转化为后续改进的能力，为金融领域智能体的纵向能力评估提供了新的基准工具。

Abstract: Most agent benchmarks evaluate tasks independently and cannot measure whether experience from one task helps with later tasks. Existing self-evolution benchmarks do not jointly cover professional workflows, open-ended deliverables, and multi-aspect evaluation. We introduce FinEvo-Bench, a longitudinal benchmark with 120 real-case-grounded tasks, 20 business scenes across six financial domains. Institution-provided professional procedures define the required operations and constraints. Eligible institution-provided and publicly documented cases supply the task facts. Each scene contains six related but substantively distinct cases that share a professional procedure and a manually reviewed rubric for task quality and financial compliance. We compare four self-evolving agent scaffolds using the same Qwen3.7-Max backbone and three independently shuffled, globally interleaved task streams. Paired non-evolving controls estimate each scaffold's self-evolution gain from retained experience, while an independent Claude Code scoring agent backed by Claude Opus 4.6 evaluates all outputs. Letta achieves the highest evolved score (91.65) and fewest compliance issues (0.09 per task); Codex achieves the largest self-evolution gain (+19.37). Across scaffolds, the evolving condition raises scores by 9.33-19.37 points and reduces compliance issues by 0.12-0.44 per task. Paired score gains at within-scene ranks 4-6 exceed those at ranks 1-3 by 6.10-8.70 points. In Claude Code, skill-only evolution produces higher task quality and fewer compliance issues than memory-only and combined memory-skill evolution. Across all four scaffolds, rubric feedback also yields higher scores and fewer compliance issues than reference-answer feedback. FinEvo-Bench measures both professional performance and self-evolution ability: how effectively an agent turns prior experience into later improvement.

</details>


### [292] [PaDoc: Layout-Grounded Parallel Decoding for Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06146)
*Hao Yu,Jiabo Zhan,Kang Liu,Linnan Zhao,Dongxu Yue,Rui Chen,Jinglin Wang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PaDoc，一种基于布局引导的端到端文档解析器，将预测的布局视为共享页面表示上的分支结构，通过前缀条件因子分解实现布局流与区域内容分支的并发推进，从而在保留全页上下文的同时消除解码依赖，显著提升吞吐量和降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端文档解析器将页面布局和区域内容序列化为单一自回归序列，导致独立区域被迫沿解码路径顺序生成且长度随总内容增长；而两阶段裁剪式解析器虽支持区域级并行，但需要重复视觉预填充且丢失分段页面上下文。两者均存在效率与上下文保真度的权衡问题。

Method: PaDoc将预测的布局视为共享页面表示上的分支结构，在区域充分性假设下推导出前缀条件因子分解，使布局流和区域内容分支并发推进。具体实现包括：在单个MLLM中使用打包的可变长度祖先注意力保持可见性并支持标准下一token训练；通过掩码并行解码创建分支，利用vLLM后端作为并发请求处理，实现缓存驻留共享前缀复用。

Result: 在OmniDocBench Full上，PaDoc实现91.1的整体布局F1；在端到端解析器中取得94.24的顶级Overall分数、最佳文本编辑距离（0.038）和公式CDM（95.59）。在384页子集和单个A800 GPU上，五个并发级别下均为最快的端到端解析器，有效页面吞吐量提升67.4%-118%，P95延迟降低39.2%-54.9%。

Conclusion: PaDoc通过布局分支结构与共享前缀复用的并行解码机制，在保持全页上下文的同时消除了区域间解码依赖，实现了准确性和效率的双重提升，验证了布局引导的因子分解方法在文档解析中的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end document parsers provide a unified interface, but serialize page layouts and regional contents into one autoregressive sequence. This formulation forces independent regions onto a decoding path whose length grows with the total content, whereas crop-based two-stage parsers expose region-level parallelism at the cost of repeated visual prefills and fragmented page context. To retain full-page context while removing dependencies, we propose PaDoc, a layout-grounded parser that treats the predicted layout as a branching structure over a shared page representation. Under a region-sufficiency assumption, we derive a prefix-conditioned factorization in which the layout stream and regional content branches advance concurrently, reducing the decoding depth to the longest layout-content path. We realize this factorization within a single MLLM: packed variable-length ancestor attention preserves the visibility under standard next-token training, while masked parallel decoding creates branches that the evaluated vLLM backend serves as concurrent requests with cache-resident shared-prefix reuse. On OmniDocBench Full, PaDoc attains an Overall layout F1 of 91.1 and, among end-to-end parsers, a top-tier Overall score of 94.24 together with the best Text Edit (0.038) and Formula CDM (95.59). On a 384-page subset and one A800 GPU, it is the fastest end-to-end parser at five concurrency levels, improving valid-page throughput by 67.4-118% and reducing P95 latency by 39.2-54.9% relative to a same-backbone Sequential SFT baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Longin-Yu/Padoc

</details>


### [293] [CogVis: Must Open-Vocabulary Change Detection Perceive the Scene Anew for Every Query?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06150)
*Zijie Wang,Chen Zhong,Wei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CogVis，一种认知记忆引导的开放词汇变化检测框架，将任务重构为"感知-记忆-验证"范式，在七个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能并提升推理吞吐量28.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇变化检测方法常将时间感知、语义判别和区域验证纠缠在一起，导致结果不稳定和冗余计算，缺乏对人类视觉变化感知机制的借鉴。

Method: 提出了认知记忆引导框架CogVis，包含三个核心组件：场景变化感知器（SCP）从冻结的双时相特征中提取可复用的、类别无关的变化先验；语义记忆校准器（SMC）通过动态估计图像-查询特定的决策阈值来补偿类别相关的分数偏移；自适应区域滤波器（ARF）利用学习的语义、时间和结构可靠性过滤连通候选区域。

Result: 在涵盖语义变化检测、二值变化定位和建筑损毁评估的七个基准数据集上均取得最先进性能，并通过共享场景级变化感知避免跨查询重复计算，推理吞吐量提升28.50%。

Conclusion: 将OVCD重构为感知-记忆-验证范式可以有效解耦时间证据与语义决策，在提升性能的同时显著提高推理效率，验证了认知记忆引导在变化检测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Earth-surface monitoring requires change detection models capable of recognizing arbitrary semantic categories. Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (OVCD) addresses this need. However, existing methods often entangle temporal perception, semantic discrimination, and region verification, causing unstable results and redundant computation. Inspired by human visual change perception, we propose CogVis, a cognitive memory-guided framework that reformulates OVCD as a perception-memory-verification paradigm. CogVis first employs a Scene Change Perceptron (SCP) to extract a reusable, category-agnostic change prior from frozen bi-temporal features, thereby decoupling temporal evidence from semantic category decisions. A Semantic Memory Calibrator (SMC) then compensates for category-dependent score shifts by dynamically estimating an image-query-specific decision threshold. Finally, an Adaptive Region Filter (ARF) filters connected candidates using learned semantic, temporal, and structural reliability. Experiments on seven benchmarks spanning semantic change detection, binary change localization, and building-damage assessment show that CogVis achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated datasets. By sharing scene-level change perception, CogVis further avoids repeating category-agnostic temporal perception across queries and improves inference throughput by 28.50%.

</details>


### [294] [iARCS: Iterative Agentic RL for Controllable 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06161)
*Saugat Adhikari,Ashok Prasad Neupane,Pramish Paudel,Ajad Chhatkuli,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iARCS是一个迭代式智能体强化学习框架，用于将预训练的3D场景生成器适配到自然语言任务需求上，通过两阶段策略（通用奖励预训练和任务特定微调）提升场景生成的功能约束保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景生成器过于注重感知真实感，却无法可靠满足任务关键的功能约束（如可达性、可通行性、空间规则合规性），这限制了合成数据在下游训练中的实用性。

Method: 采用两阶段策略：第一阶段进行通用奖励预训练，提升物理合理性和布局质量；第二阶段利用LLM生成的奖励程序进行任务特定微调，并根据训练反馈迭代优化奖励程序。

Result: 实验表明在可步行性、可达性和净空等任务上的约束保真度显著提升，实现了有效的任务特定约束优化，同时保持了有竞争力的场景多样性。生成的合成数据还能进一步改善基础生成器。

Conclusion: iARCS不仅是可控的场景编辑方法，更是一个实用的合成数据生成工具，能够弥合感知真实感与功能约束之间的差距，提升合成数据在下游任务中的价值。

Abstract: Synthetic 3D scene generation is increasingly used as a data source for computer vision and embodied AI, but existing generators often optimize perceptual realism without reliably satisfying task-critical functional constraints. This mismatch limits the usefulness of synthetic data for downstream training, where accessibility, traversability, and spatial rule compliance are often essential. We present iARCS, an iterative agentic reinforcement learning framework that adapts a pretrained scene generator to natural-language task requirements. iARCS uses a two-stage strategy: universal-reward pretraining to improve physical plausibility and layout quality, followed by task-specific fine-tuning with LLM-generated reward programs that are iteratively refined from training feedback. Experiments show improved constraint fidelity on walkability, reachability, and clearance-focused tasks, effective task-specific constraint optimization, and competitive scene diversity. We further show that data generated by iARCS improves a base generator, supporting its value as a practical synthetic data generation tool rather than only a controllable scene editing method.

</details>


### [295] [MicroEvo: Knowledge-Guided LLM Sampling for Efficient Microarchitecture Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06183)
*Jia Xiong,Runkai Li,Chenxu Niu,Guangyuan Gao,Changwen Xing,Yifan Zhang,Xinlai Wan,Jieran Cui,Chen Bai,Yusheng Hua,Ying Wang,Ming Ling,Xi Wang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroEvo，一个知识引导的框架，将LLM与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）相结合，用于多目标微架构优化。通过LLM驱动的进化算子、帕累托感知的树策略、主动知识积累机制和状态感知指令，MicroEvo在帕累托前沿质量上比NSGA-II提升高达36.2%，搜索效率提升10.6倍，并展现出对工业级复杂核的良好可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微架构设计空间探索面临搜索空间庞大和PPA评估代价高昂的问题，现有方法进行盲目搜索，未考虑微架构依赖关系，也无法从迭代搜索中有效学习，导致评估浪费和帕累托收敛性差。

Method: MicroEvo框架包含四个核心组件：LLM驱动的进化算子（利用LLM生成智能的微观架构变异）、帕累托感知的树策略（在帕累托贡献和多样性之间取得平衡）、主动知识积累机制（提取并复用优化洞见）以及状态感知指令（在线自适应调整搜索行为）。

Result: 实验表明，MicroEvo相比NSGA-II在帕累托前沿质量上提升高达36.2%，搜索效率提升10.6倍，并且在复杂的工业级核心上展现出强大的可扩展性。代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: MicroEvo有效解决了微架构设计空间探索中盲目搜索和知识浪费的问题，通过LLM与MCTS的深度耦合实现了更高效、更高质量的多目标优化，为工业级微架构设计提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Microarchitecture design space exploration suffers from expansive search spaces and expensive PPA evaluation, leaving only a small simulation budget for design decision-making. Existing methods perform blind search without considering microarchitectural dependencies and fail to learn from the iterative search effectively, leading to wasted evaluations and weak Pareto convergence. In this paper, we propose MicroEvo, a knowledge-guided framework that couples off-the-shelf LLMs with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) for multi-objective microarchitecture optimization. MicroEvo combines LLM-driven evolutionary operators, a Pareto-aware tree policy that balances Pareto contribution and diversity, an active knowledge accumulation mechanism that extracts and reuses optimization insights, and state-aware directives that adapt the search behavior online. Experiments show that MicroEvo improves Pareto-front quality by up to 36.2% over NSGA-II and achieves 10.6x higher search efficiency, and also demonstrates strong scalability to a complex industrial-scale core. The code repository is available at: https://github.com/GEAR-SEU/MicroEvo-ICCAD-26.

</details>


### [296] [Comparative Approaches to Agent Retrieval over Large Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06196)
*Indivara Kolluru,Nathan Sportsman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型技能库中技能检索与排序的问题，对比了混合排序器和类型化知识图谱两种方法。结果表明，混合排序器在117个真实查询中top-5命中率达73.5%，而知识图谱不仅没有带来改进，反而显著更差（-11.2点）。原因是图谱的候选边来源于与排序器相同的嵌入邻域，无法扩展检索范围。作者还指出使用作者编写的查询评估会高估hit@5高达44点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型技能库驱动的智能体需要在海量技能中决定加载哪些技能以及执行顺序，将整个技能库加载到上下文中代价高昂且缺乏结构化排序能力，因此需要高效的技能检索与排序方法。

Method: 在690个技能的语料库上对比两个系统：(1) 结合词法检索和稠密嵌入检索的混合排名器（稀疏、按需加载）；(2) 编码先决条件、数据流和顺序等工作流关系的类型化知识图谱。在117个真实非回声查询上进行评估，并对图谱邻居替换、预过滤拓扑边界以及评估偏差等问题进行机制性分析。

Result: 混合排名器在top-5中正确检索技能的比例为73.5%±8.0；图谱方法显著更差（-11.2点，p=0.0007），其LLM生成的边层没有增加价值，73%排名器未命中的查询在图谱中完全不可达；98.6%的类型化边连接的是排名器已共同浮出的技能；使用作者编写的查询评估会高估hit@5达44点。

Conclusion: 知识图谱虽然能丰富关系语义，但不能扩展检索覆盖面，因为其候选边来源于排名器已搜索的相同嵌入邻域（预过滤拓扑边界）。论文提供了"为何添加结构不能改进强排名器检索效果"的机制性解释，并指出了在何种条件下向检索中添加结构依赖性是最优的。

Abstract: Agents backed by large skill libraries must decide which skills to load and in what order. Loading the entire library into context is expensive and provides no structure for autonomous sequencing. We study two systems for this problem over a corpus of 690 skills: a hybrid ranker combining lexical and dense-embedding retrieval for sparse, on-demand loading, and a typed knowledge graph encoding workflow relations such as prerequisites, data flow, and ordering. On a set of 117 realistic, non-echoing queries, the hybrid ranker retrieves the correct skill within the top five in 73.5% +/- 8.0 of cases, leaving roughly a quarter of queries unserved. When used as the design intended (substituting graph neighbours for additional ranked results at matched token budget), the graph is significantly worse (-11.2 points, p = 0.0007). Its LLM-generated edge layer adds nothing over neighbours obtained free from a local embedding pass, and 73% of the queries the ranker misses are not reachable through the graph at all. We attribute this to a pre-filter topology bound. Because the graph's candidate edges are drawn from the same embedding neighbourhood the ranker already searches, 98.6% of typed edges connect skills the ranker had already surfaced together. The graph can enrich relation semantics but cannot extend retrieval reach. We further show that evaluating on author-written queries overstates hit@5 by up to 44 points, which would have hidden these results entirely. Our contribution is a mechanistic account of why added structure does not improve retrieval over a strong ranker, and identify the conditions under which adding structural interdependence into the retrieval is optimal.

</details>


### [297] [EnvACE: Internalizing Environment Dynamics via World Rehearsal for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06197)
*Zishan Xu,Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Yifu Guo,Zhengxi Lu,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Xingshan Zeng,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnvACE提出了一种名为"世界排练"（world rehearsal）的智能体强化学习方法，训练过程中用策略自身模拟环境响应来替代真实或合成可执行环境的外部交互，从而实现无需外部环境的长时程工具使用智能体训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大型语言模型智能体进行长时程工具使用通常依赖真实或合成的可执行环境，这些环境的构建和验证成本高昂；外部模拟器又难以接地。这些限制促使研究者探索无需外部环境交互的训练新路径。

Method: EnvACE的策略在"行动"与"排练"之间交替：先生成工具调用，然后扮演环境角色产生该动作引发的响应，后续决策基于排练得到的响应。两个角色通过任务成功奖励进行端到端联合优化，使策略将动作与环境响应之间的关系内化到参数中，形成智能体世界模型。

Result: 在BFCL-v4、tau^2-Bench、VitaBench和FinMCP-Bench四个基准上，EnvACE取得了强劲且可迁移的性能，在整体评估中优于环境扩展基线。受控研究表明世界排练在不同模型规模下均能持续改善策略学习。测试阶段，内化的世界模型支持在正式执行前进行私下排练，在适度排练预算下无需额外外部交互即可获得性能提升。

Conclusion: 世界排练为扩展LLM智能体训练提供了一条超越外部环境约束的新路径，证明了智能体可以将环境模型内化到自身参数中，从而摆脱对外部环境交互的依赖。

Abstract: Training large language model agents for long-horizon tool use typically relies on interactions with real or synthesized executable environments, whose construction and verification are costly, or on external simulators that are difficult to ground. We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [298] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-RAG框架，将检索增强生成(RAG)技术应用于时间序列预测任务。通过引入专门的参考token来融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列的信息，解决了传统时间序列模型因数据量有限、参数规模小而不适用直接拼接参考序列的问题。实验证明TS-RAG在多个真实世界预测基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG在大型语言模型中已被证明能通过引入外部相关信息提升模型能力，但在时间序列预测领域应用有限。现有时间序列模型受限于训练数据不足、参数规模小和缺乏生成能力，简单模仿语言模型的方式拼接参考序列并不能获得理想效果，因此需要专门设计适合时间序列预测的RAG框架。

Method: 提出TS-RAG框架，核心创新在于设计专门的参考token（reference tokens），用于有效融合输入序列与检索得到的相似时间序列之间的信息。该方法不是简单地将参考序列拼接到输入中，而是通过专门设计的token机制实现信息融合，从而更好地捕捉复杂的时间动态特征。

Result: 在多个真实世界的时间序列预测基准数据集上，TS-RAG取得了一致的最优性能（state-of-the-art），证明了所提方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过为时间序列预测量身定制的参考token设计，RAG技术可以成功应用于该领域，有效弥补了时间序列模型在数据量和参数规模上的不足，为提升预测精度提供了新的思路。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [299] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DASH（Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons）方法，通过将局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值的差距映射为自适应的传播门控，并用于控制反向多步聚合，从而自适应地调整token级监督权重，以改进基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中标准的on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）方法对时序结构利用不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR使用自动可验证的结果信号改善大语言模型的推理能力，但这类信号通常是稀疏的且是序列级的。OPSD通过查询特权教师来提供密集的token级分布监督以缓解信号稀疏问题，但标准OPSD未能充分利用rollout的时间结构——它对每个局部散度赋予相同的系数，忽略了散度在生成过程中所处的位置及其历史序列。由于局部标量无法区分时间上下文，标准OPSD无法根据实际的散度序列调整token级权重。

Method: DASH将每个局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值之间的差距映射为自适应的传播门控（propagation gate），然后利用这些门控控制反向多步聚合（backward multi-step aggregation）。通过这种方式，DASH能够根据生成过程中局部散度的演化情况来调整token级监督权重。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准和三种模型规模上的实验表明，DASH在所有基准和所有规模上都优于匹配的vanilla OPSD基线。此外，DASH复用了OPSD已计算的教师和学生分布，增益不需要额外的教师或学生前向传播。

Conclusion: DASH通过自适应调整token级监督权重，有效解决了标准OPSD对散度时间上下文利用不足的问题，在不增加计算成本的情况下显著提升了模型推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [300] [Improving the Realism of Synthetic Clinical Benchmarks Under Utility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06265)
*Omid Bazgir,Md Nasir,Jacob Hoffman,Yang Yang,Manu Agrawal,Anusua Trivedi,Vinay Rao Dandin,Chris Gibbons,Christine Swisher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何在保持下游效用检查不失败的前提下，提升企业AI代理合成临床基准数据的结构现实性。通过在针对护理缺口（care-gap）的Synthea基准上提出"效用约束下的现实性改进"框架，作者发现基线基准存在严重的结构不现实问题（如缺失率高、可操作行数少、token高度集中），并提出两种确定性修订方法在效用边界内显著提升现实性指标。研究还发现基准内部现实性与对聚合运营参考数据的源保真度是不同目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐私敏感的医疗场景中，运营数据难以获取，合成临床基准虽然能通过现有的效用检查，但结构上仍可能不现实。现有做法仅以效用检查作为充分条件，缺乏对合成基准现实性的显式优化，这可能导致企业AI代理在合成环境中表现良好但在实际应用中失效。

Method: 将基准修订形式化为"效用约束下的现实性改进"问题：数据修改需提升现实性且不跌破运营效用下限。在由Synthea生成的护理缺口基准上，通过演示EHR工作流和下游处理流水线实例化该方法。现实性通过四个维度测量：缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性、人群对齐。提出两种确定性修订策略，并以朴素致密化作为对照。

Result: 基线基准存在严重结构缺陷：采样对缺失率79.44%，仅12.75%的行可操作，38.94%的患者无可操作测量项，前三token集中度达100%。两种确定性修订在保持效用下限的同时显著改善了现实性面板，而朴素致密化对照仍保留了不现实的模板化结构。内部基准现实性与源保真度被证明是相关但不同的目标。

Conclusion: 合成基准质量应被显式优化，效用应被视为一个约束条件而非现实性的充分证据。这一发现对隐私敏感场景下企业AI代理的可信评估具有重要意义，提示需要专门的现实性优化方法与度量体系。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [301] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果审计框架来评估多模态大语言模型在"以图思考"范式中的视觉工具使用是否有因果效力。通过在策略、轨迹和步骤三个层面进行干预，作者发现视觉工具使用存在两种失败模式（"只看不调用"和"调用不规划"），并揭示了"视觉工具使用的幻觉"现象：尽管聚合精度有提升，但在广泛的轨迹上视觉工具使用并非因果有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM使用裁剪、缩放等视觉操作时，往往相比直接推理只有边际甚至负面的收益，同时消耗更高的token成本，并可能反复裁剪无关区域、在直接推理能正确回答的问题上失败。因此需要严格检验返回的视觉证据是否真正因果地影响了答案。

Method: 将视觉工具使用形式化为一个因果图，分离观察介导路径与行动诱导捷径。在三个层面进行干预审计：（1）策略层面：比较工具使用与直接推理；（2）轨迹层面：在rollout过程中损坏所有观察；（3）步骤层面：在固定前缀下反事实替换单个观察。提出步骤级估计量"视觉证据增益"（Visual Evidence Gain）来隔离每个返回观察的贡献。

Result: 在六个代表性模型和五个细粒度感知基准上，发现了策略校准不当的两种失败模式："只看不调用"（返回的观察对答案无因果效应）和"调用不规划"（观察有信息但调用调度不连贯）。轨迹级诊断分解出策略级精度增益，发现增益集中于少数"校准"子集。

Conclusion: 提出"视觉工具使用的幻觉"这一概念：尽管聚合精度有提升，视觉工具使用在广泛的轨迹上并非因果有效。作者开源了CauAudit代码以供进一步研究。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [302] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuanTiMedAI框架，结合智能体大语言模型（LLM）进行临床特征选择与紧凑量子循环网络进行时间感知的ICU心脏骤停死亡率预测，仅用605个参数即在MIMIC-IV队列上达到0.852的AUROC，比当前最优基线提升约2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏骤停在ICU中致死率极高，但现有预测研究多依赖入院早期的静态摘要，忽略了患者整个ICU住院期间生理恶化与恢复的时间动态变化。这一局限性促使研究者开发能够捕捉时序演变信息的预测方法。

Method: 提出QuanTiMedAI框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）智能体LLM用于临床知情特征发现与选择；（2）紧凑型量子循环网络用于时序感知的死亡率预测，通过非线性特征增强在保持极低参数量的同时实现高效建模。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV心脏骤停患者队列上，QuanTiMedAI仅用605个参数达到0.852的AUROC，相比当前最先进基线提升约2.9%。智能体LLM引导的特征选择优于传统特征选择方法，量子架构以极低参数量取得竞争力预测性能。消融实验系统验证了各架构设计选择的贡献。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明量子增强的序列建模能够在参数数量大幅减少的情况下超越经典循环网络，为ICU心脏骤停死亡率预测提供了一种高效、精准的新范式，同时验证了智能体LLM在临床特征发现中的价值。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [303] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了概念激活向量（CAV）方法，用于分析基于Transformer的L2英语口语自动评分系统（BERT文本评分器和基于Whisper的多模态评分器）中对无关说话者属性（如母语和年龄）的偏见，并探索了稀疏自编码器（SAE）能否在复杂神经嵌入空间中提供更清晰的概念方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动口语评估系统在高风险场景中日益普及，其评分必须仅依赖于口语能力而非说话者的无关属性（如母语、年龄）。然而，基于Transformer的评分模型是黑盒结构，使得公平性和可解释性分析变得困难。现有CAV方法主要适用于基于特征的评分器，需要将其扩展到神经评分系统。

Method: 1) 将CAV分析扩展到两个神经口语评估系统：基于BERT的文本评分器和基于Whisper的语音-文本多模态评分器；2) 使用CAV表示人类可解释的概念在模型激活空间中的方向，区分概念是否被编码与是否影响预测分数（后者通过基于梯度的敏感性度量）；3) 探索使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在稀疏潜空间学习CAV并映射回激活空间，以解决复杂神经嵌入空间中线性可分性不足的问题。

Result: 1) 概念的可恢复性强烈依赖于被探测的表征和架构，而非仅取决于概念本身；2) 概念敏感性也具有架构依赖性；3) SAE使概念在线性可恢复性方面有所改善，但在低维层中会削弱原始激活空间中的敏感性。

Conclusion: 在审计口语评估系统中的偏见时，必须区分概念的可恢复性与概念的影响力，因为SAE虽然提高了概念的可恢复性，却可能掩盖概念对评分的实际影响，这两个维度需要分别进行评估。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [304] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajDebug框架，用于在LLM智能体系统的长轨迹中定位关键错误步骤，并构建了含486条人工标注失败轨迹的TrajErrBench基准，实验表明其诊断结果能有效提升下游智能体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统在复杂领域表现优异，但存在级联错误和调试困难的问题。现有的关键错误检测方法面临两大挑战：一是长轨迹中证据分散、难以定位单个错误；二是失败轨迹往往包含多个局部错误，难以确定哪些错误对最终失败负有责任。

Method: 提出TrajDebug错误生命周期追踪框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 多粒度历史压缩与基于证据的错误识别，解决长轨迹中的错误发现；(2) 通过追踪每个错误的解决状态和末端影响进行关键归因。同时构建TrajErrBench基准，包含486条来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的人工标注失败轨迹。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上的实验表明，TrajDebug在整体性能上优于现有基线方法；应用研究进一步证明其诊断结果能为下游智能体改进提供可操作的反馈并提升成功率。

Conclusion: TrajDebug通过错误生命周期追踪有效解决了长轨迹中的关键错误检测与归因问题，且诊断结果具有实际应用价值。作者将公开代码和数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [305] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对可解释人工智能（XAI）评估不足的问题展开研究，以DetoxAI图像识别系统为例说明偏见检测和概念遗忘中的XAI局限性，提出了人类为中心的评估方法，并探讨了在概念漂移下自适应生成的解释方法，最后关联了数据、模型与解释共同演化追踪的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释人工智能（XAI）方法在评估方面存在不足，缺乏充分的验证手段。作者希望通过具体的应用案例（DetoxAI系统）来说明这些局限性，并探索更完善的评估和自适应解释方法。

Method: 本文通过DetoxAI图像识别系统演示XAI评估的局限性，提出人类为中心的图像分类解释评估方法，并探索在数据流概念漂移条件下自适应调整反事实解释的方法。

Result: 展示了XAI评估不足的具体案例，提出了人类参与的评估框架，并讨论了将反事实解释适配到概念漂移场景的实践经验。

Conclusion: XAI评估需要更严谨的方法论支持，而解释系统必须具备适应动态数据流的能力，同时需要建立追踪数据、模型与解释共同演化的机制。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [306] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于可执行事件轨迹的参量化视频基准测试方法，用于诊断事件计数任务中的时序推理失败模式。通过在2,190个受控视频中变化事件数量N和频率F，并附带可执行事件轨迹作为真值，作者发现模型在瞬态事件（如眨眼）上完全没有可靠的计数区域，而在持续性状态转换上最多可可靠计数12个事件。增加采样率虽能提升最终分数（19.6%→29.3%），但报告序列与真实事件轨迹的一致性仅3.7%，表明额外帧会虚增得分但不产生忠实的事件恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实视频基准的固定片段会混淆事件数量、频率、时长和视觉复杂度等因素，难以隔离失败模式；而程序化基准仅评分最终答案，无法审计模型报告的事件是否与可执行的真值轨迹一致。因此需要一种既能精确控制参数、又能提供细粒度事件级诊断的评估方法。

Method: 提出trace-grounded parametric profiling方法，在三种受控视频任务（弹球墙壁接触、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换）中生成2,190个视频，系统性地变化事件数量N和频率F，保持渲染固定。每个视频附带一个可执行事件轨迹，支持能力曲面估计和时间戳级别评估。

Result: 在80%可靠性阈值下，Gemini 3.6 Flash能可靠计数最多12个持续性状态转换（0.5和1.0 Hz），但对瞬态眨眼事件没有任何可靠的正向计数区域。在高数量高频率区域，仅0.2%的最终计数正确，模型仅恢复18.1%的真实事件。增加采样率将弹球任务准确率从19.6%提升至29.3%，但报告序列与真值轨迹一致率仅3.7%。不同提示策略收益有限，真实视频评估同样显示出低事件数量下成功集中的现象。

Conclusion: 事件表征方式决定了模型能否初始访问证据，该限制会随着数量和频率的增加而放大。trace-grounded profiling将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转变为详细的时序推理失败诊断，有助于定位模型在哪些条件下、哪些事件类型上失效。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [307] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），一种基于证据链接和评分标准（rubric）的自动化心力衰竭电子健康记录（EHR）特征工程流水线，在500条虚拟患者记录上生成132个结构化特征和70个评分聚合特征，使HFrEF和HFpEF表型分类的AUROC分别从0.895提升至0.963和从0.870提升至0.910，特征证据支持与方法学健全性评分达最高分的81.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR特征工程占数据科学家39-45%的工作量，是临床研究和AI的主要瓶颈。心力衰竭影响了约670万美国成年人，其EHR数据碎片化且需要结合疾病特异性的指南临床推理。现有基于规则和LLM的方法只能部分自动化，缺乏可维护性和证据可追溯性，亟需更完善的自动化方案。

Method: 开发了nMAS（Nimblemind多智能体系统），这是一个证据链接、评分标准驱动的自动化特征工程流水线。系统在500条来自九个EHR源表的虚拟患者记录上运行，生成132个结构化特征和70个评分聚合特征，并通过受限LLM进行审计，验证结构完整性、评分标准符合性和来源可追溯性。

Result: 加入聚合特征后，HFrEF表型分类的留出集AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910。独立LLM评分评估显示，特征在证据支持和方法学健全性方面达到最高分的81.5%。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在复杂心血管EHR数据上实现自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性，但评估仅限于单机构队列，需要外部验证来进一步确认方法的普适性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [308] [Towards a Risk Assessment of Malicious Skill Files in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05223)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自主编码代理智能体技能接口的对抗性攻击方法，通过LLM将真实shell命令转化为看似良性的技能文件，并在两个企业级代理（Gemini CLI和Qwen Code）上验证了极高的攻击成功率（最高96.1%），揭示了技能接口的严重安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理在企业软件工作流中越来越普及，其技能接口（文件夹中的指令和脚本）被动态加载以定制行为，但同时扩大了攻击面——恶意shell命令可以隐藏在看似无害的自然语言技能文件中。现有研究缺乏对这种攻击的系统性评估，企业面临未知的安全风险。

Method: （1）提出对抗性技能合成方法，使用四大家族六个LLM将471个真实shell命令转化为看似良性的技能，生成2,826个技能的基准测试集，映射到11个MITRE ATT&CK战术；（2）构建可复现评估流水线，结合运行分层、证据锚定、拒绝否决和确定性声明意图覆盖，配合三人LLM评审委员会，经人工金标准验证（Cohen's kappa=0.85）；（3）在两个企业级代理上进行了5,629次完整运行的大规模特征化评估。

Result: Gemini CLI在95.5-96.1%的运行中被成功利用，Qwen Code在71.6-74.0%的运行中被利用，结果对生成模型几乎不敏感；显式安全识别仅发生在1.99%的运行中。代码和数据集已公开。

Conclusion: 企业在采用编码代理之前必须评估并缓解技能接口风险。技能接口作为攻击面存在严重安全缺陷，现有代理对隐藏恶意指令的检测能力极弱。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly embedded in enterprise software workflows with delegated authority over connected systems. Central to this architecture is the agent skills interface: folders of instructions and scripts that agents load dynamically to specialize their behavior. This interface also widens the attack surface, letting malicious shell commands hide within natural-language skill files. We make three contributions. First, an adversarial skill-synthesis method using six LLMs across four families to transform 471 real-world shell commands into benign-appearing skills, released as a benchmark of 2,826 skills mapped to 11 MITRE ATT&CK tactics. Second, a reproducible evaluation pipeline coupling run stratification, evidence anchoring, a refusal veto, and a deterministic declared-intent override with a three-judge LLM-as-a-judge panel, validated against a blind human gold standard (Cohen's kappa = 0.85). Third, a large-scale characterization of two enterprise-grade agents across 5,629 completed runs. Gemini CLI is exploited in 95.5-96.1% of runs and Qwen Code in 71.6-74.0% (raw majority vote to declared-intent-corrected estimate, both within the human gold standard), nearly invariant to the generating model. Explicit safety recognition occurs in only 1.99% of runs. Enterprises must assess and mitigate skill-interface risk before adopting coding agents. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/awsm-research/AgentJailbreak

</details>


### [309] [Improving Debugging in Verification-Aware Languages Through Automated Fault Localization: A Case Study in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05399)
*Álvaro Silva,Isabel Amaral,João Pascoal Faria,Alexandra Mendes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了验证感知语言（如Dafny）中的自动化故障定位方法，对比了基于状态和基于反例两种范式。通过MutDafny生成变异数据集并使用EXAM评分评估，发现基于反例的方法显著优于基于状态的方法，其中单迹的结构化排序提升最大，多迹聚合进一步增强鲁棒性和调试效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证感知语言在验证失败时提供的反馈通常仅限于特定错误条件（如违反后置条件），而非根本原因。虽然Dafny的反例功能提供具体执行轨迹，但每条断言失败通常只暴露单一失败路径，开发者仍需手动遍历整个轨迹来定位错误，缺乏自动化故障定位支持。

Method: 论文对比两种故障定位范式：(1) 基于状态的方法，复制AutoFix的"快照"方法论，通过推断不变量和谓词来识别可疑程序状态；(2) 基于反例的方法，包含一系列逐步丰富验证器输出的技术：从原始反例提取，到结构化单迹排序，再到多迹聚合。评估框架使用MutDafny从DafnyBench生成多样化变异数据集，并用EXAM分数衡量定位效果。

Result: 基于反例的方法在实验设置中显著优于基于状态的方法。单迹上的结构化排序相比原始反例输出带来最大改进，而多迹聚合通过增加覆盖率和减少求解器引入的路径偏差，进一步提升了鲁棒性和调试效用。

Conclusion: 在验证感知语言中，有效的故障定位既依赖于使用反例信息，也取决于如何结构化组织和多样化这些信息。这为验证感知语言中自动化调试工具的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Verification-aware languages, like Dafny, integrate formal specifications directly into source code to enable static correctness checks. However, when verification fails, the feedback provided is often limited to the specific condition of the error, such as a violated postcondition, rather than the root cause of the fault. While Dafny's counterexample features provide concrete execution traces, these typically expose a single failing path per assertion failure, leaving the developer to manually look through the entire trace to locate the error.
  This paper investigates automated fault localization for verification-aware languages by comparing two paradigms: state-based and counterexample-based localization. Our state-based localization strategy replicates the ``snapshot'' methodology of AutoFix by inferring invariants and predicates to identify suspicious program states. The counterexample-based strategy consists of a family of techniques that progressively enrich the use of verifier output: from raw counterexample extraction, to structured single-trace ranking, and to multi-trace aggregation.
  To validate these methods, we present an evaluation framework using MutDafny to generate a diverse mutant dataset from DafnyBench and measure localization effectiveness using the EXAM score. Our results show that counterexample-based approaches substantially outperform state-based localization in this setting. Structured ranking over a single trace yields the largest improvement over raw counterexample output, while multi-trace aggregation provides additional gains in robustness and debugging utility by increasing coverage and reducing path bias introduced by the solver. These findings demonstrate that effective fault localization in verification-aware languages depends both on using counterexample information, and how that information is structured and diversified.

</details>


### [310] [Reasoning from Traces: Divergence-Guided Agentic Repair of WebAssembly Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05521)
*Liyan Huang,Kaicheng Wang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出WasmMend，这是首个自动修复Native-Wasm功能不一致的系统。它通过差分轨迹分析定位函数级别的首次执行分歧点，并引导LLM代理推理根因并生成补丁，在真实C/C++项目上实现了70.0%的修复率，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly承诺能够将C/C++代码库无缝复用为可移植、快速、沙箱化的二进制文件，但实际中跨编译到Wasm和原生二进制常常导致运行时行为不一致，根源在于平台运行时层面且被源代码掩盖，即使是先进的基于LLM的修复代理也难以修复这些差异。

Method: WasmMend采用两阶段方法：首先通过新颖的差分轨迹分析方法定位Wasm和原生执行首次出现分歧的函数；然后以此定位为指导，让LLM代理推理根本原因并生成能够消除分歧行为的补丁。

Result: 在真实C/C++项目上的实验显示，WasmMend达到70.0%的修复率，而基于代理的基线方法为50.2%，结合修复时LLM插桩的增强方法为54.5%。
